\doxysection{zbs\+::ZSearch\+Entity Class Reference}
\hypertarget{classzbs_1_1_z_search_entity}{}\label{classzbs_1_1_z_search_entity}\index{zbs::ZSearchEntity@{zbs::ZSearchEntity}}


The \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_search_entity}{ZSearch\+Entity} class generic entity class that may hold both file entity OR collection entity. Both may transparently be used and accessed as if it refers to a file.

\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
The \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_search_entity}{ZSearch\+Entity} class generic entity class that may hold both file entity OR collection entity. Both may transparently be used and accessed as if it refers to a file. 

a \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_collection_entity}{ZCollection\+Entity} is a single collection entit\texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} with
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item an array of \doxylink{class_u_r_f_parser}{URFParser} objects, one per entity in the entities set
\item an associated dictionary pointing to dictionary of selected entities \+: if selected entity is a file entity, dictionary is file dictionary
\item a join clause to link entities between each others this clause is omitted if only one entity is in the selected entities set this clause must exist if more than one entity is in the selected entities set
\item a selection clause to select records from each entity of the set
\item a number of records to display 
\end{DoxyItemize}

A \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_search_entity}{ZSearch\+Entity} has no name, its name is stored within either \+\_\+\+Base\+File\+Entity or \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_collection_entity}{ZCollection\+Entity}.

A \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_search_entity}{ZSearch\+Entity} is a set of one or more base entities with
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item an array of \doxylink{class_u_r_f_parser}{URFParser} objects, one per base entity in the entities set
\item an associated dictionary pointing to dictionary of selected entities \+: if selected entity is a file entity, dictionary is file dictionary
\item a join clause to link any of base entities for entity set between each others this clause is omitted if only one entity is in the selected base entities set this clause must exist if more than one entity is in the selected base entities set
\item a selection clause to select records from each of base entity within the set
\item a number of resulting records to display as first found records
\end{DoxyItemize}

a \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_collection_entity}{ZCollection\+Entity} is a single collection entit\texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} with
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item an array of \doxylink{class_u_r_f_parser}{URFParser} objects, one per entity in the entities set
\item an associated dictionary pointing to dictionary of selected entities \+: if selected entity is a file entity, dictionary is file dictionary
\item a join clause to link entities between each others this clause is omitted if only one entity is in the selected entities set this clause must exist if more than one entity is in the selected entities set
\item a selection clause to select records from each entity of the set
\item a number of records to display 
\end{DoxyItemize}

A \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_search_entity}{ZSearch\+Entity} has no name, its name is stored within either \+\_\+\+Base\+File\+Entity or \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_collection_entity}{ZCollection\+Entity}.

A \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_search_entity}{ZSearch\+Entity} is a set of one or more base entities with
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item an array of \doxylink{class_u_r_f_parser}{URFParser} objects, one per base entity in the entities set
\item an associated dictionary pointing to dictionary of selected entities \+: if selected entity is a file entity, dictionary is file dictionary
\item a join clause to link any of base entities for entity set between each others this clause is omitted if only one entity is in the selected base entities set this clause must exist if more than one entity is in the selected base entities set
\item a selection clause to select records from each of base entity within the set
\item a number of resulting records to display as first found records 
\end{DoxyItemize}
